<script>
(function() {
    var sel = document.getElementById("locale-currency-selector");
    if (!sel) return;
    var trigger = sel.querySelector(".locale-currency-selector__trigger");
    var dropdown = document.getElementById("locale-currency-dropdown");
    function hrefWithCurrency(href, code) {
        if (typeof airhintCurrencyHrefWithParam === "function") return airhintCurrencyHrefWithParam(href, code);
        try {
            var u = new URL(href, window.location.origin);
            if (code) u.searchParams.set("currency", code);
            return u.pathname + u.search + u.hash;
        } catch (e) {
            return href;
        }
    }
    function getCurrentCurrency() {
        if (typeof currencyManager !== "undefined" && currencyManager && typeof currencyManager.getCurrentCurrency === "function")
            return currencyManager.getCurrentCurrency();
        if (typeof airhintResolveStoredCurrencyPreference === "function") {
            var r = airhintResolveStoredCurrencyPreference();
            if (r) return r;
        }
        return "EUR";
    }
    function open() {
        if (currSelect) currSelect.value = getCurrentCurrency();
        dropdown.hidden = false;
        trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
    }
    function close() {
        dropdown.hidden = true;
        trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    }
    function toggle() {
        if (dropdown.hidden) open(); else close();
    }
    trigger.addEventListener("click", function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); toggle(); });
    document.addEventListener("click", function(e) { if (!dropdown.hidden && !sel.contains(e.target)) close(); });
    document.addEventListener("keydown", function(e) { if (e.key === "Escape") close(); });
    var panel = sel.querySelector(".locale-currency-selector__panel");
    var currentLocale = panel ? panel.getAttribute("data-current-locale") || "en" : "en";
    var langSelect = document.getElementById("locale-currency-lang-select");
    var currSelect = document.getElementById("locale-currency-curr-select");
    function applyAndClose() {
        var loc = langSelect ? langSelect.value : currentLocale;
        var c = currSelect ? currSelect.value : null;
        if (c) {
            if (typeof currencyManager !== "undefined" && currencyManager) currencyManager.setCurrency(c);
            else if (typeof persistPreferredCurrency === "function") persistPreferredCurrency(c);
        }
        var localeChanged = langSelect && loc !== currentLocale;
        if (localeChanged) {
            try { localStorage.setItem("preferred_locale", loc); } catch (e) {}
            if (loc === "en") document.cookie = "preferred_locale=en; path=/; max-age=31536000; SameSite=Lax";
            var base = langSelect.getAttribute("data-path-without-locale") || "/";
            var tail = langSelect.getAttribute("data-path-tail") || "";
            var noLocalePrefix = langSelect.getAttribute("data-no-locale-prefix") === "true";
            var url = (loc === "en" || noLocalePrefix) ? base : "/" + loc + (tail ? "/" + tail : "");
            if (noLocalePrefix && loc !== "en") document.cookie = "preferred_locale=" + loc + "; path=/; max-age=31536000; SameSite=Lax";
            window.location.href = c ? hrefWithCurrency(url, c) : url;
        } else if (c) {
            var hasData = typeof store !== "undefined" && store.origin && store.destination;
            if (hasData && typeof triggerSearch === "function") {
                var dep = store.departureDate || (typeof $ !== "undefined" && $("#departure").val());
                var ret = store.returnDate || (typeof $ !== "undefined" && $("#return_date").val());
                triggerSearch(store.origin, store.destination, dep, ret, c);
                if (typeof loadEarlyHints !== "undefined") setTimeout(function() { loadEarlyHints(store.origin, store.destination, dep, ret, null, c); }, 200);
            } else {
                var urlHasCurrencyParam = false;
                try {
                    urlHasCurrencyParam = new URL(window.location.href).searchParams.has("currency");
                } catch (e) {}
                var dealsListing =
                    typeof window.__AIRHINT_DEALS_PAGE !== "undefined" && window.__AIRHINT_DEALS_PAGE;
                if (dealsListing || urlHasCurrencyParam) {
                    window.location.href = hrefWithCurrency(
                        window.location.pathname + window.location.search + window.location.hash,
                        c
                    );
                } else {
                    window.location.reload();
                }
            }
        }
        close();
    }
    var saveBtn = document.getElementById("locale-currency-save");
    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.addEventListener("click", applyAndClose);
    var cancelBtn = document.getElementById("locale-currency-cancel");
    if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.addEventListener("click", close);
})();
</script>

                <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
                    <path d="M20 21V19C20 17.9391 19.5786 16.9217 18.8284 16.1716C18.0783 15.4214 17.0609 15 16 15H8C6.93913 15 5.92172 15.4214 5.17157 16.1716C4.42143 16.9217 4 17.9391 4 19V21M16 7C16 9.20914 14.2091 11 12 11C9.79086 11 8 9.20914 8 7C8 4.79086 9.79086 3 12 3C14.2091 3 16 4.79086 16 7Z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path>
                </svg>

<script>
    // Add click handler for profile icon (authenticated users)
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
        const profileIcon = document.getElementById("navbar-user-profile");
        const loginIcon = document.getElementById("open-auth-modal");
        const userContainer = document.getElementById("navbar-user-container");

        // Detect native app
        const isNativeApp = navigator.userAgent.includes('AirHint-Native') ||
            window.location.href.includes('capacitor://') ||
            document.referrer.includes('capacitor://') ||
            (navigator.userAgent.includes('Android') && navigator.userAgent.includes('wv'));

        if (isNativeApp && userContainer) {
            // Initially hide auth UI for native apps while waiting for NATIVE_VERSION
            userContainer.style.display = 'none';

            // Check if NATIVE_VERSION already arrived before this listener was set up
            // (handles race condition where native responds before DOMContentLoaded)
            if (window.pushHandler && window.pushHandler.supportsNativeAuth === true) {
                userContainer.style.display = '';
            }
        }

        // Add click handlers (always add them, visibility controlled separately)
        if (profileIcon) {
            profileIcon.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (typeof openAuthModal === "function") {
                    openAuthModal();
                }
            });
        }

        if (loginIcon) {
            loginIcon.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (typeof openAuthModal === "function") {
                    openAuthModal();
                }
            });
        }

        // Listen for nativeAuthDetected event to show/hide based on NATIVE_VERSION
        window.addEventListener('nativeAuthDetected', function(event) {
            if (userContainer) {
                if (event.detail.supported === true) {
                    // Modern app with auth support - show auth UI
                    userContainer.style.display = '';
                } else {
                    // Legacy app or no support - keep hidden
                    userContainer.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<h2>Porquê o AirHint?</h2>
<p class="faq_item">
    Porque a nossa tecnologia com IA ajuda-o a poupar e a encontrar o melhor momento para reservar voos! Se voar com companhias low-cost, os nossos algoritmos garantem que obtém um bilhete verdadeiramente barato. Com IA treinada em milhões de alterações de preços, o AirHint analisa padrões complexos e indica o melhor momento para comprar bilhetes com precisão baseada em dados.
</p>

<h2>Quando é o melhor momento para reservar voos?</h2>
<p class="faq_item">
    Os padrões de preços variam muito entre companhias e rotas. A sabedoria popular diz para reservar cedo, mas a nossa investigação com IA mostra que não é sempre o ideal—especialmente em low-cost com preços dinâmicos. O previsor do AirHint analisa flutuações em tempo real. As nossas redes processam milhões de dados para recomendar o melhor momento com mais de 80% de precisão.
    </p><ul>
        <li><a href="/articles" target="_blank">Leia a nossa investigação com IA sobre os voos mais baratos</a></li>
    </ul>

<p></p>

<h2>Qual é o melhor dia para reservar voos?</h2>
<p class="faq_item">
    Investigámos e confirmámos que há tendências de melhor dia da semana/mês, mas varia por companhia. Para algumas companhias pode encontrar artigos detalhados sobre o melhor dia para reservar:
    </p><ul>
        <li><a href="/articles/best-time-to-buy-airline-tickets" target="_blank">O melhor momento para comprar bilhetes de avião</a></li>
        <li><a href="/ryanair/best-weekday" target="_blank">O melhor dia da semana para comprar bilhetes Ryanair</a></li>
        <li><a href="/southwest/about" target="_blank">Quando comprar bilhetes Southwest baratos</a></li>
        <li><a href="/easyjet/about" target="_blank">Quando comprar bilhetes easyJet baratos</a></li>
    </ul>

<p></p>

<h2>Previsor de preços de voos com IA</h2>
<p class="faq_item">
    O nosso previsor de preços usa aprendizagem automática para mostrar intervalos de preço, níveis de confiança e probabilidades para o melhor momento de reserva. Os nossos algoritmos indicam se deve reservar já ou esperar, com probabilidades de descida de preço. A IA do AirHint ajuda-o a voar barato com previsões precisas e ofertas exclusivas!
</p>

<h4>Porque é que a nossa IA é única?</h4>
<p class="faq_item">
    A nossa arquitetura treina redes neuronais por companhia aérea, com precisão de previsão muito superior. Enquanto a concorrência usa modelos genéricos, o nosso sistema adapta-se ao comportamento de preços de cada companhia. O resultado: mais de 80% de precisão face à média de 60-70%. A nossa IA aprende com cada alteração de preço para poupanças reais!
</p>

<p class="faq_item">
    Saiba mais sobre o nosso <a href="/flight-price-tracker">rastreador e previsor de preços de voos</a>.
</p>

<h4>Porque é que as companhias aéreas mudam os preços tão frequentemente?</h4>
<p class="faq_item">
    As companhias usam algoritmos de gestão de rendimento que otimizam preços consoante popularidade do voo, sazonalidade, férias escolares, eventos e oferta e procura. As low-cost ajustam tarifas várias vezes por dia. A IA do AirHint foi concebida para estar à frente desses algoritmos! Os nossos modelos analisam milhões de alterações de preços para prever movimentos com precisão científica.
</p>

<h4>Onde posso ver as melhores ofertas por rota?</h4>
<p class="faq_item">
    A nossa <a href="/pt/deals/origins">página de ofertas de voos mais baratos</a> página é atualizada regularmente com as melhores ofertas para as rotas mais populares.
</p>

<h4>Como posso reservar um voo?</h4>
<p class="faq_item">
    Trabalhamos com afiliados de reservas (OTA) para bilhetes. Para voos e outras low-cost, os nossos parceiros permitem reservar o voo escolhido e mostram alternativas.
</p>


<h2>Confiar ou não confiar</h2>
<p class="faq_item">
    Fazemos o possível para merecer a sua confiança. Oferecemos recomendações estatísticas e há uma margem de erro. As companhias, sobretudo low-cost, alteram preços frequentemente e por vezes ninguém consegue prever o comportamento exato. Não é possível garantir 100% de precisão. No entanto, pistas objetivas e informadas são possíveis—e é isso que oferecemos. O AirHint é a sua melhor opção para encontrar ofertas e o melhor preço para o seu voo.
</p>

<script>
// Push notification state
let pushEnabled = false;
let emailEnabled = true;
let isUserAuthenticated = false;
let isEmailVerified = false;

// Check if user is authenticated and email is verified
async function checkAuthStatus() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/auth/status', { credentials: 'include' });

        if (!response.ok) {
            console.error('Auth status check failed with status:', response.status);
            return false;
        }

        const data = await response.json();
        let isAuth = Boolean(data.authenticated && data.user);

        // Store email verification status
        if (isAuth && data.user) {
            isEmailVerified = Boolean(data.user.email_verified);
        } else {
            isEmailVerified = false;
        }

        // Session expired but native app may still have auth — attempt recovery
        if (!isAuth && window.pushHandler?.isNativeApp && typeof window.ensureFreshToken === 'function') {
            console.log('Not authenticated, attempting native session recovery...');
            await window.ensureFreshToken();

            // Re-check after recovery attempt
            const retryResponse = await fetch('/auth/status', { credentials: 'include' });
            if (retryResponse.ok) {
                const retryData = await retryResponse.json();
                isAuth = Boolean(retryData.authenticated && retryData.user);
                if (isAuth && retryData.user) {
                    isEmailVerified = Boolean(retryData.user.email_verified);
                    console.log('✓ Session recovered, authenticated:', isAuth, 'verified:', isEmailVerified);
                }
            }
        }

        // Session expired but web browser may still have Firebase auth — attempt recovery
        if (!isAuth && !window.pushHandler?.isNativeApp && typeof firebaseAuth !== 'undefined') {
            const currentUser = firebaseAuth.currentUser;
            if (currentUser) {
                console.log('Not authenticated on server but Firebase user exists, rebuilding session...');
                setRecoveryLoadingState(true);
                try {
                    const idToken = await currentUser.getIdToken(true);
                    const loginResponse = await fetch('/sessionLogin', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        credentials: 'include',
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            idToken: idToken,
                            displayName: currentUser.displayName || ''
                        })
                    });

                    if (loginResponse.ok) {
                        const retryResponse = await fetch('/auth/status', { credentials: 'include' });
                        if (retryResponse.ok) {
                            const retryData = await retryResponse.json();
                            isAuth = Boolean(retryData.authenticated && retryData.user);
                            if (isAuth && retryData.user) {
                                isEmailVerified = Boolean(retryData.user.email_verified);
                                console.log('✓ Web session recovered, authenticated:', isAuth);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    console.warn('Web session recovery failed:', error);
                } finally {
                    setRecoveryLoadingState(false);
                }
            }
        }

        return isAuth;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error checking auth status:', error);
        return false;
    }
}

// Enable/disable notification toggles based on auth and verification status
function setTogglesAuthState(authenticated) {
    isUserAuthenticated = authenticated;

    // Check if we should show auth gate (not in legacy native app)
    const shouldShowAuth = shouldShowAuthGate();

    if (authenticated && !isEmailVerified) {
        // User is authenticated but email not verified - show verification gate
        $('#auth-gate-section').hide();
        $('#verification-gate-section').show();

        // Disable toggles
        $('#email_toggle').prop('disabled', true).prop('checked', false);
        $('#push_toggle').prop('disabled', true).prop('checked', false);
        $('#email_toggle_container').addClass('disabled');
        $('#push_toggle_container').addClass('disabled');

        // Hide notification sections
        $('#email_section').hide();
        $('#push_section').removeClass('show');

        // Update state
        emailEnabled = false;
        pushEnabled = false;
    } else if (authenticated && isEmailVerified) {
        // User is authenticated and verified - allow alert creation
        $('#auth-gate-section').hide();
        $('#verification-gate-section').hide();

        // Enable toggles
        $('#email_toggle').prop('disabled', false);
        $('#push_toggle').prop('disabled', false);
        $('#email_toggle_container').removeClass('disabled');
        $('#push_toggle_container').removeClass('disabled');

        // Auto-fill email from user account and make read-only
        fetchUserEmailAndFill();
    } else if (shouldShowAuth) {
        // User not authenticated - show auth gate
        $('#auth-gate-section').show();
        $('#verification-gate-section').hide();

        // Disable toggles
        $('#email_toggle').prop('disabled', true).prop('checked', false);
        $('#push_toggle').prop('disabled', true).prop('checked', false);
        $('#email_toggle_container').addClass('disabled');
        $('#push_toggle_container').addClass('disabled');

        // Hide notification sections
        $('#email_section').hide();
        $('#push_section').removeClass('show');

        // Update state
        emailEnabled = false;
        pushEnabled = false;
    } else {
        // Native app - hide auth gate, continue with legacy device-based flow
        $('#auth-gate-section').hide();
        $('#verification-gate-section').hide();

        // Enable toggles for legacy device-based flow
        $('#email_toggle').prop('disabled', false);
        $('#push_toggle').prop('disabled', false);
        $('#email_toggle_container').removeClass('disabled');
        $('#push_toggle_container').removeClass('disabled');

        // Show push toggle container and enable push for native app
        $('#push_toggle_container').show();
        $('#push_toggle').prop('checked', true);

        // Show email section for legacy flow (ensure email field is editable)
        $('#email_section').show();
        $('#alert_email').prop('readonly', false).css('background-color', '');
        emailEnabled = true;
        pushEnabled = true;
    }
}

// Determine if auth gate should be shown
function shouldShowAuthGate() {
    // Auth disabled via feature flag - use legacy flow
    

    // In browser: always show web auth
    if (!window.pushHandler?.isNativeApp) {
        return true;
    }

    // In native app: check if auth is supported
    // null (no response/old app) or false = legacy flow, no auth gate
    // true = modern app, show auth gate
    return window.pushHandler?.supportsNativeAuth === true;
}

// Show/hide recovery loading state during web session recovery
function setRecoveryLoadingState(show) {
    if (show) {
        $('#auth-gate-section').hide();
        $('#verification-gate-section').hide();
        $('#recovery-loading-section').show();
    } else {
        $('#recovery-loading-section').hide();
    }
}

// Fetch user email from session and auto-fill
async function fetchUserEmailAndFill() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/auth/status', { credentials: 'include' });
        const data = await response.json();

        if (data.authenticated && data.user && data.user.email) {
            const userEmail = data.user.email;
            $('#alert_email').val(userEmail);
            $('#alert_email').prop('readonly', true);
            $('#alert_email').css('background-color', '#f5f5f5');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error fetching user email:', error);
    }
}

// Handle sign-in button click
$('#auth-gate-signin-btn').on('click', function() {
    // Save full alert context so it survives page reloads (popup blocked, redirect fallback)
    sessionStorage.setItem('pendingPriceAlert', JSON.stringify({
        origin: store.origin,
        destination: store.destination,
        departureDate: store.departureDate,
        departureTime: store.departureTime,
        returnDate: store.returnDate,
        flight_number: store.flight_number,
        airline: store.airline,
        currency: store.currency,
        direct: store.direct,
        affiliate: store.affiliate,
        price: parseFloat($('#price_raw').val()),
        directOnly: $('#directOnly').prop('checked'),
        tripType: getTripType()
    }));
    
    // Close the alert dialog first
    $('#create_alert_dialog').modal('hide');
    
    // Use native login if app supports it, otherwise web auth modal
    if (window.pushHandler && window.pushHandler.supportsNativeAuth === true) {
        console.log('Triggering native login from alert dialog');
        window.pushHandler.requestNativeLogin();
    } else {
        // Trigger web auth modal
        console.log('Triggering web auth modal from alert dialog');
        if (typeof openAuthModal === 'function') {
            // Small delay to ensure alert dialog is fully closed
            setTimeout(function() {
                openAuthModal();
            }, 300);
        }
    }
});

// Restore store fields and search filter from a saved pending alert context
function restorePendingAlertContext(ctx) {
    store.origin = ctx.origin;
    store.destination = ctx.destination;
    store.departureDate = ctx.departureDate;
    store.departureTime = ctx.departureTime;
    store.returnDate = ctx.returnDate;
    store.flight_number = ctx.flight_number;
    store.airline = ctx.airline;
    store.currency = ctx.currency;
    store.direct = ctx.direct;
    store.affiliate = ctx.affiliate;
    if (ctx.price) {
        $('#price_raw').val(ctx.price);
    }
    // Restore the directOnly search filter so any auto-triggered search preserves it
    if (typeof ctx.directOnly !== 'undefined') {
        $('#directOnly').prop('checked', ctx.directOnly);
    }
}

// Listen for auth state changes (when user signs in without page reload)
window.addEventListener('authStateChanged', async function(event) {
    if (event.detail.authenticated) {
        console.log('Auth state changed - user signed in');

        // Update email verification status from event
        isEmailVerified = Boolean(event.detail.email_verified);

        // Update toggle states for authenticated user
        await setTogglesAuthState(true);

        // Check if we have a pending alert dialog to reopen
        var pending = sessionStorage.getItem('pendingPriceAlert');
        if (pending) {
            sessionStorage.removeItem('pendingPriceAlert');

            // Restore the full alert context (guards against store being stale or wiped)
            try {
                var ctx = JSON.parse(pending);
                if (ctx && ctx.origin) {
                    restorePendingAlertContext(ctx);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                // Legacy format ('true') — no context to restore
            }

            // Small delay to ensure auth modal is fully closed
            setTimeout(function() {
                $('#create_alert_dialog').modal('show');
            }, 300);
        }
    }
});

// Listen for email verification success (when user verifies without page reload)
window.addEventListener('emailVerified', async function(event) {
    if (event.detail.verified) {
        console.log('Email verified - updating dialog state');

        // Update verification status
        isEmailVerified = true;

        // Update toggle states to show form instead of verification gate
        await setTogglesAuthState(true);
    }
});


// Update toggle container styling
function updateToggleContainer(toggleId, enabled) {
    const container = $(`#${toggleId}_toggle_container`);
    if (enabled) {
        container.addClass('enabled');
    } else {
        container.removeClass('enabled');
    }
}

// Update push status display
function updatePushStatus(message, type) {
    const statusElement = $('#push_status');
    const textElement = $('#push_status_text');
    
    statusElement.removeClass('info success error warning').addClass(type);
    textElement.text(message);
}

// Handle toggle changes
$('#email_toggle').change(function() {
    emailEnabled = $(this).is(':checked');
    updateToggleContainer('email', emailEnabled);
    
    if (emailEnabled) {
        $('#email_section').show();
        $('#alert_email').prop('required', true);
    } else {
        $('#email_section').hide();
        $('#alert_email').prop('required', false);
        // Remove email from localStorage when user explicitly disables email notifications
        localStorage.removeItem('airhint_user_email');
    }
});

$('#push_toggle').change(function() {
    const isChecked = $(this).is(':checked');
    
    if (isChecked) {
        // Check if we're in native app
        if (!window.pushHandler?.isNativeApp) {
            $("#alert-error").html("Push notifications are only available in the Android app.");
            $("#alert-error").show();
            $(this).prop('checked', false);
            return;
        }
        
        // Request permission and token when push is enabled
        if (typeof pushHandler !== 'undefined') {
            pushHandler.requestPermissionForDialog();
        } else {
            $(this).prop('checked', false);
            return;
        }
    } else {
        pushEnabled = false;
        updateToggleContainer('push', false);
        $('#push_section').removeClass('show');
        $('#alert-error').hide();
    }
});

// Dynamic form height adjustment for radio buttons
$('input[name="alert_precision"]').change(function() {
    const selectedValue = $(this).val();
    const form = $('#alert_precision_form');
    
    if (selectedValue === 'cheapest') {
        // Expand form to accommodate toggle with proper spacing
        form.css('height', '100px');
        form.css('margin-bottom', '15px');
    } else {
        // Collapse form to minimal height
        form.css('height', '60px');
        form.css('margin-bottom', '0px');
    }
});

// Toast notification functions
function showToast(message, type = 'success', duration = 5000, linkText = null, linkUrl = null) {
    const container = $('#toast-container');
    const toastId = 'toast-' + Date.now();
    
    let linkHtml = '';
    if (linkText && linkUrl) {
        linkHtml = `<a href="${linkUrl}" class="toast-link" target="_blank">${linkText}</a>`;
    }
    
    const toastHtml = `
        <div class="toast ${type}" id="${toastId}">
            <div class="toast-content">
                <div class="toast-message">
                    ${message}
                    ${linkHtml}
                </div>
                <button class="toast-close" onclick="closeToast('${toastId}')" aria-label="Close notification">×</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
    
    container.append(toastHtml);
    
    // Show the toast with animation
    setTimeout(() => {
        $(`#${toastId}`).addClass('show');
    }, 100);
    
    // Auto-dismiss after duration
    if (duration > 0) {
        setTimeout(() => {
            closeToast(toastId);
        }, duration);
    }
}

function closeToast(toastId) {
    const toast = $(`#${toastId}`);
    toast.removeClass('show');
    setTimeout(() => {
        toast.remove();
    }, 300);
}

// Direct flights toggle doesn't need container state management

// Initialize when dialog is shown
$('#create_alert_dialog').on('shown.bs.modal', async function() {
    // Check authentication status first
    const authenticated = await checkAuthStatus();
    console.log('Auth status check:', authenticated, 'Email verified:', isEmailVerified);

    // If authenticated but not verified, check if user might have verified recently
    if (authenticated && !isEmailVerified) {
        console.log('User authenticated but not verified, checking if they verified recently...');
        if (typeof window.checkEmailVerification === 'function') {
            const verified = await window.checkEmailVerification();
            if (verified) {
                console.log('Email verification detected! Updating dialog state...');
                isEmailVerified = true;
            }
        }
    }

    setTogglesAuthState(authenticated);

    // Listen for late NATIVE_VERSION response in case it arrives after dialog opens
    const nativeAuthHandler = async function(event) {
        if (event.detail.supported === true) {
            if (!authenticated) {
                // Modern native app with auth support, user not authenticated — re-check
                // in case session recovery happened between dialog open and version response
                const recheckAuth = await checkAuthStatus();
                setTogglesAuthState(recheckAuth);
            }
            // If authenticated, no action needed — dialog is already in correct state
        }
        window.removeEventListener('nativeAuthDetected', nativeAuthHandler);
    };

    if (window.pushHandler?.isNativeApp && window.pushHandler?.supportsNativeAuth === null) {
        window.addEventListener('nativeAuthDetected', nativeAuthHandler);
    }

    // If not authenticated AND showing auth gate (web browser), skip the rest of initialization
    // For native apps, we continue to set up the push toggle properly
    if (!authenticated && shouldShowAuthGate()) {
        console.log('User not authenticated, showing auth gate');
        return;
    }

    console.log('User authenticated, continuing with normal initialization');
    
    // Check if there's an error message - if so, don't reset toggles
    const hasError = $('#alert-error').is(':visible') && $('#alert-error').html().trim() !== '';
    
    // Check if we're coming from an error by looking for existing toggle states
    const hasExistingState = $('#email_toggle').is(':checked') || $('#push_toggle').is(':checked');
    
    // If there's an error, preserve current toggle settings
    if (hasError) {
        emailEnabled = $('#email_toggle').is(':checked');
        pushEnabled = $('#push_toggle').is(':checked');
        
        // Update UI based on current state
        updateToggleContainer('email', emailEnabled);
        updateToggleContainer('push', pushEnabled);
        
        if (emailEnabled) {
            $('#email_section').show();
            $('#alert_email').prop('required', true);
        } else {
            $('#email_section').hide();
            $('#alert_email').prop('required', false);
        }
        
        // Auto-fill email if previously saved
        const savedEmail = localStorage.getItem('airhint_user_email');
        if (savedEmail && emailEnabled) {
            $('#alert_email').val(savedEmail);
        }
        
        return; // Exit early, don't reset toggles
    }
    
    // Check if we're in mobile app with push notifications available
    let isInNativeApp = false;
    
    // First, try to use pushHandler if it's available and initialized
    if (typeof pushHandler !== 'undefined' && pushHandler.isNativeApp !== undefined) {
        isInNativeApp = pushHandler.isPushAvailable();
    } else {
        // Fallback detection for AirHint native app
        const isAirHintNative = navigator.userAgent.includes('AirHint-Native');
        const isCapacitor = window.location.href.includes('capacitor://') || document.referrer.includes('capacitor://');
        const isAndroidWebView = /Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent) && /wv/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
        
        // Only enable push if we're in the actual AirHint app, not just any Android WebView
        if (isAirHintNative || isCapacitor) {
            isInNativeApp = true;
        }
    }
    
    // Set initial states based on environment (no animation)
    let initialEmailEnabled = !isInNativeApp; // Enable email in web browser, disable in native app
    let initialPushEnabled = isInNativeApp;   // Enable push in native app, disable in web browser
    
    if (isInNativeApp) {
        // For native app, check if there's a saved email from previous alerts
        const savedEmail = localStorage.getItem('airhint_user_email');
        if (savedEmail && savedEmail.trim() !== '') {
            initialEmailEnabled = true;
            initialPushEnabled = true; // Keep push enabled too
        } else {
            initialEmailEnabled = false;
            initialPushEnabled = true;
        }
    } else if (hasExistingState && !hasError) {
        // For existing state, use current values but ensure they're valid for environment
        initialEmailEnabled = $('#email_toggle').is(':checked');
        initialPushEnabled = $('#push_toggle').is(':checked');
    } else {
        // For web browser, always start with email enabled and push disabled
        initialEmailEnabled = true;
        initialPushEnabled = false;
    }
    
    emailEnabled = initialEmailEnabled;
    pushEnabled = initialPushEnabled;
    
    // Set email toggle state immediately
    $('#email_toggle').prop('checked', initialEmailEnabled);
    updateToggleContainer('email', initialEmailEnabled);
    
    // Push toggle state will be set after we determine if it should be visible
    
    // Use the new PushNotificationHandler if available - CHECK THIS FIRST!
    if (typeof pushHandler !== 'undefined' && pushHandler.isNativeApp !== undefined) {
        pushEnabled = pushHandler.isPushAvailable();
        
        // Show push toggle container for native app
        $('#push_toggle_container').show();
        
        // Now set the push toggle state
        $('#push_toggle').prop('checked', pushEnabled);
        updateToggleContainer('push', pushEnabled);
        
        // Call initializeForAlertDialog when the modal is actually shown
        if (typeof pushHandler.initializeForAlertDialog === 'function') {
            try {
                pushHandler.initializeForAlertDialog();
            } catch (error) {
                // Handle error silently
            }
        }
    } else {
        // Fallback to more specific environment detection
        const isAirHintNative = navigator.userAgent.includes('AirHint-Native');
        const isCapacitor = window.location.href.includes('capacitor://') || document.referrer.includes('capacitor://');
        
        if (isAirHintNative || isCapacitor) {
            $('#push_toggle_container').show();
            updatePushStatus('App notifications available in native Android app', 'info');
            pushEnabled = true;
            
            // Set the push toggle state
            $('#push_toggle').prop('checked', pushEnabled);
            updateToggleContainer('push', pushEnabled);
        } else {
            // Hide push toggle if no mobile app detected
            $('#push_toggle_container').hide();
            updatePushStatus('App notifications only available in the Android app', 'info');
            pushEnabled = false;
            
            // Set the push toggle state
            $('#push_toggle').prop('checked', pushEnabled);
            updateToggleContainer('push', pushEnabled);
        }
    }
    
    // Show/hide email section based on current state
    if (emailEnabled) {
        $('#email_section').show();
        $('#alert_email').prop('required', true);
    } else {
        $('#email_section').hide();
        $('#alert_email').prop('required', false);
    }
    
    // Auto-fill email if previously saved
    const savedEmail = localStorage.getItem('airhint_user_email');
    if (savedEmail && emailEnabled) {
        $('#alert_email').val(savedEmail);
    }
    
    // Direct flights toggle is simple, no state management needed
    
    // Toggles are now always visible, no animation needed
});

// Add validation for notification types
function validateNotificationSettings() {
    const emailChecked = $('#email_toggle').is(':checked');
    const pushChecked = $('#push_toggle').is(':checked');
    
    // Validate email if required
    if (emailChecked && !$('#alert_email').val()) {
        $("#alert-error").html("Email is required for email notifications.");
        $("#alert-error").show();
        return false;
    }
    
    // Validate push is available if required
    if (pushChecked) {
        // Check if we're in native app and have FCM token
        if (!window.pushHandler?.isNativeApp) {
            $("#alert-error").html("App notifications are only available in the Android app.");
            $("#alert-error").show();
            return false;
        }
        
        if (!window.pushHandler?.fcmToken) {
            $("#alert-error").html("App notifications are not enabled. Please enable them first.");
            $("#alert-error").show();
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Ensure at least one notification method is selected
    if (!emailChecked && !pushChecked) {
        $("#alert-error").html("Please enable at least one notification method (email or push).");
        $("#alert-error").show();
        return false;
    }
    
    // Clear any previous errors
    $("#alert-error").hide();
    return true;
}

// Handle enable push notifications button click
$(document).on('click', '#enable_push_btn', function() {
    if (typeof pushHandler !== 'undefined') {
        pushHandler.requestPermissionForDialog();
    }
});

// Handle successful push permission/token
function onPushEnabled() {
    pushEnabled = true;
    updateToggleContainer('push', true);
    $('#push_toggle').prop('checked', true);
    $('#push_status').text('Enabled');
    $('#push_section').addClass('show');
}

// Handle alert precision radio button changes to show/hide direct flights toggle
$(document).ready(function() {
    // Check if user just signed in and should reopen alert dialog (page reload fallback)
    const pendingAlert = sessionStorage.getItem('pendingPriceAlert');
    if (pendingAlert) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem('pendingPriceAlert');

        // Restore the full alert context before any auto-search triggers
        try {
            var ctx = JSON.parse(pendingAlert);
            if (ctx && ctx.origin) {
                restorePendingAlertContext(ctx);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            // Legacy format ('true') — no context to restore
        }

        // Wait a moment for page to fully load, then open the dialog
        setTimeout(function() {
            $('#create_alert_dialog').modal('show');
        }, 500);
    }
    
    // Initially hide the direct flights toggle since "Current flight only" is checked by default
    $('.extra_settings').hide();
    
    // Handle radio button changes
    $('input[name="alert_precision"]').change(function() {
        if ($(this).val() === 'cheapest') {
            // Show direct flights toggle when "Cheapest flight for the day" is selected
            $('.extra_settings').slideDown(200);
        } else {
            // Hide direct flights toggle when "Current flight only" is selected
            $('.extra_settings').slideUp(200);
        }
    });
    

});

// Clean up modal backdrop when dialogs are hidden
$('#create_alert_dialog').on('hidden.bs.modal', function () {
    // Remove any lingering backdrop elements
    $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
    $('body').removeClass('modal-open');
});

$('#alert_success_dialog').on('hidden.bs.modal', function () {
    // Remove any lingering backdrop elements
    $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
    $('body').removeClass('modal-open');
});

$('#unsubscribe_success_dialog').on('hidden.bs.modal', function () {
    // Remove any lingering backdrop elements
    $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
    $('body').removeClass('modal-open');
});

// Handle resend verification email button
$('#resend-verification-btn').on('click', function() {
    if (typeof window.resendVerificationEmail === 'function') {
        window.resendVerificationEmail();
    } else {
        alert('Please sign in to resend verification email.');
    }
});

// Handle check verification button
$('#check-verification-btn').on('click', async function() {
    if (typeof window.checkEmailVerification === 'function') {
        const verified = await window.checkEmailVerification();
        if (verified) {
            // Update state and re-enable form
            isEmailVerified = true;
            setTogglesAuthState(true);
        }
    } else {
        alert('Please sign in to verify your email.');
    }
});
</script>

<script>
(function() {
    // Stepper button click handler
    $('#travelersSheet .btn-stepper').on('click', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var fieldId = $(this).attr('data-field');
        var type = $(this).attr('data-type');
        var $input = $('#' + fieldId);
        var currentVal = parseInt($input.val());
        var min = parseInt($input.attr('min'));
        var max = parseInt($input.attr('max'));

        if (isNaN(currentVal)) {
            currentVal = min;
        }

        if (type === 'minus' && currentVal > min) {
            $input.val(currentVal - 1);
        } else if (type === 'plus' && currentVal < max) {
            $input.val(currentVal + 1);
        }

        updateStepperButtons(fieldId);
    });

    function updateStepperButtons(fieldId) {
        var $input = $('#' + fieldId);
        var currentVal = parseInt($input.val());
        var min = parseInt($input.attr('min'));
        var max = parseInt($input.attr('max'));

        var $minus = $('#travelersSheet .btn-stepper[data-type="minus"][data-field="' + fieldId + '"]');
        var $plus = $('#travelersSheet .btn-stepper[data-type="plus"][data-field="' + fieldId + '"]');

        $minus.prop('disabled', currentVal <= min);
        $plus.prop('disabled', currentVal >= max);
    }

    // Initialize button states when sheet opens
    $('#travelersSheet').on('show.bs.modal', function() {
        updateStepperButtons('sheet-adults');
        updateStepperButtons('sheet-children');
        updateStepperButtons('sheet-infants');
    });

    // Clean up backdrop on close (Bootstrap 3 quirk)
    $('#travelersSheet').on('hidden.bs.modal', function() {
        $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
        $('body').removeClass('modal-open');
        // Close dropdown when sheet closes
        $('#cabin-dropdown-trigger').removeClass('open');
        $('#cabin-dropdown-menu').removeClass('open');
    });

    // Custom dropdown handling
    $('#cabin-dropdown-trigger').on('click', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        $(this).toggleClass('open');
        $('#cabin-dropdown-menu').toggleClass('open');
    });

    $('#cabin-dropdown-menu .dropdown-item').on('click', function() {
        var value = $(this).data('value');
        var text = $(this).text();

        // Update hidden input and display text
        $('#sheet-cabin').val(value);
        $('#cabin-dropdown-text').text(text);

        // Update selected state
        $('#cabin-dropdown-menu .dropdown-item').removeClass('selected');
        $(this).addClass('selected');

        // Close dropdown
        $('#cabin-dropdown-trigger').removeClass('open');
        $('#cabin-dropdown-menu').removeClass('open');
    });

    // Close dropdown when clicking outside
    $(document).on('click', function(e) {
        if (!$(e.target).closest('.custom-dropdown').length) {
            $('#cabin-dropdown-trigger').removeClass('open');
            $('#cabin-dropdown-menu').removeClass('open');
        }
    });
})();
</script>

<script>
    (function() {
        // Fallback OS detection function (in case detectOperatingSystem is not available)
        function detectOS() {
            if (typeof detectOperatingSystem === 'function') {
                return detectOperatingSystem();
            }
            // Fallback detection
            const userAgent = navigator.userAgent;
            if (userAgent.includes('Android') || userAgent.includes('AirHint-Native-Android')) {
                return 'android';
            }
            if (userAgent.includes('iPhone') || userAgent.includes('iPad') || userAgent.includes('AirHint-Native-iOS')) {
                return 'ios';
            }
            return 'desktop';
        }
        
        // Format currency amount; number format uses locale/country (country→BCP 47, matches api.js)
        var _numberFormatLocaleMap = { "US": "en-US", "GB": "en-GB", "IE": "en-IE", "IN": "en-IN", "AU": "en-AU", "NZ": "en-NZ", "CA": "en-CA", "PH": "en-PH", "SG": "en-SG", "ZA": "en-ZA", "AE": "ar-AE", "SA": "ar-SA", "IL": "he-IL", "PR": "en-US", "AR": "es-AR", "MX": "es-MX", "ES": "es-ES", "CO": "es-CO", "CL": "es-CL", "DE": "de-DE", "FR": "fr-FR", "IT": "it-IT", "RU": "ru-RU", "BR": "pt-BR", "PT": "pt-PT", "PL": "pl-PL", "CH": "de-CH", "AT": "de-AT", "NL": "nl-NL", "BE": "nl-BE", "TR": "tr-TR", "JP": "ja-JP", "CN": "zh-CN", "EN": "en-GB", "en": "en-GB", "es": "es-ES", "it": "it-IT", "ru": "ru-RU", "pt": "pt-PT", "pl": "pl-PL" };
        function formatCurrencyAmount(amount, currency) {
            var n = Math.round(parseFloat(amount));
            var locale = (document.getElementById("user_country") && document.getElementById("user_country").value) || window.__LOCALE;
            if (locale) { locale = _numberFormatLocaleMap[locale] || _numberFormatLocaleMap[locale.toUpperCase()] || (locale.length === 2 ? locale + "-" + locale.toUpperCase() : "en-GB"); }
            try {
                var formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(locale || "en-GB", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(n);
            } catch (e) {
                formatted = String(n).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
            }
            currency = currency.toUpperCase();
            
            if (currency === 'EUR') {
                return formatted + ' ' + '\u20AC';
            } else if (currency === 'GBP') {
                return '\u00A3' + ' ' + formatted;
            } else if (currency === 'USD') {
                return '\u0024' + ' ' + formatted;
            } else if (currency === 'INR') {
                return '\u20B9' + ' ' + formatted;
            } else if (currency === 'MXN') {
                return 'MX$ ' + formatted;
            } else if (currency === 'ARS') {
                return 'AR$ ' + formatted;
            } else if (currency === 'CHF') {
                return 'Fr. ' + formatted;
            } else if (currency === 'AED') {
                return 'AED ' + formatted;
            } else if (currency === 'BRL') {
                return 'R$ ' + formatted;
            } else {
                return formatted + ' ' + currency;
            }
        }
        
        // Check for rate_app query parameter
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const rateApp = urlParams.get('rate_app');
        
        // Show modal if rate_app parameter is present (accepts '1', 'true', or any truthy value)
        if (rateApp && (rateApp === '1' || rateApp.toLowerCase() === 'true')) {
            // Wait for DOM to be ready
            $(document).ready(function() {
                // Detect operating system
                const userOS = detectOS();
                const container = $('#app_store_button_container');
                
                // Check for saved_amount query parameter
                const savedAmount = urlParams.get('saved_amount');
                const savedCurrency = urlParams.get('saved_currency');
                
                // Get current currency if saved_currency not provided
                let currencyToUse = savedCurrency;
                if (!currencyToUse) {
                    // Try to get currency from currencyManager if available
                    if (typeof currencyManager !== 'undefined' && typeof currencyManager.getCurrentCurrency === 'function') {
                        currencyToUse = currencyManager.getCurrentCurrency();
                    } else if (typeof getUserCurrency === 'function') {
                        currencyToUse = getUserCurrency();
                    } else {
                        // Fallback to EUR
                        currencyToUse = 'EUR';
                    }
                }
                
                // Display savings if amount is provided
                if (savedAmount && !isNaN(parseFloat(savedAmount))) {
                    const savingsContainer = $('#savings_message_container');
                    const savingsAmountDisplay = $('#savings_amount_display');
                    const ratingMessage = $('#rating_modal_message');
                    
                    // Format and display savings
                    const formattedAmount = formatCurrencyAmount(savedAmount, currencyToUse);
                    savingsAmountDisplay.text(formattedAmount);
                    savingsContainer.show();
                    
                    // Update message to mention savings
                    ratingMessage.html('Your feedback helps us improve. Please rate us on the app store.');
                }
                
                // Get user country from DOM (default to 'us')
                const userCountryElement = document.getElementById('user_country');
                const userCountry = userCountryElement ? (userCountryElement.value || 'us').toLowerCase() : 'us';
                
                // App store URLs
                const androidStoreUrl = 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.airhint.app';
                const iosStoreUrl = `https://apps.apple.com/${userCountry}/app/airhint/id6754238703`;
                
                // Show appropriate app store button based on OS
                if (userOS === 'android') {
                    container.html(`
                        <a href="${androidStoreUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" 
                           class="app-store-button" 
                           style="display: inline-block; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: middle;">
                            <img src="/static/img/GetItOnGooglePlay_Badge_Web_color_English.png" 
                                 alt="Get it on Google Play" 
                                 style="height: 60px; width: auto; border: none; display: block;"/>
                        </a>
                    `);
                } else if (userOS === 'ios') {
                    container.html(`
                        <a href="${iosStoreUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" 
                           class="app-store-button" 
                           style="display: inline-block; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: middle;">
                            <img src="/static/img/Download_on_the_App_Store_Badge_US-UK_RGB_blk_092917.svg" 
                                 alt="Download on the App Store" 
                                 style="height: 60px; width: auto; border: none; display: block;"/>
                        </a>
                    `);
                } else {
                    // Desktop or unknown - show both buttons
                    container.html(`
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: center; gap: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                            <a href="${androidStoreUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" 
                               class="app-store-button" 
                               style="display: inline-block; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: middle;">
                                <img src="/static/img/GetItOnGooglePlay_Badge_Web_color_English.png" 
                                     alt="Get it on Google Play" 
                                     style="height: 60px; width: auto; border: none; display: block;"/>
                            </a>
                            <a href="${iosStoreUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" 
                               class="app-store-button" 
                               style="display: inline-block; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: middle;">
                                <img src="/static/img/Download_on_the_App_Store_Badge_US-UK_RGB_blk_092917.svg" 
                                     alt="Download on the App Store" 
                                     style="height: 60px; width: auto; border: none; display: block;"/>
                            </a>
                        </div>
                    `);
                }
                
                // Show modal with a slight delay for better UX
                setTimeout(function() {
                    $('#app_rating_modal').modal('show');
                }, 500);
                
                // Track app store button clicks (optional analytics)
                $(document).on('click', '.app-store-button', function() {
                    // You can add analytics tracking here if needed
                    // Example: gtag('event', 'app_rating_click', { 'platform': userOS });
                });
                
                // Clean up modal backdrop when closed
                $('#app_rating_modal').on('hidden.bs.modal', function() {
                    $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
                    $('body').removeClass('modal-open');
                });
            });
        }
    })();
</script>

    </div>
